# Roadmap — AssessIQ (open work; renamed 2026-10-15 from `PENDING_TASKS_2026-10-01.md`)

*Type: reference* — This page lists the open work, the status board and the task rows.

## Status board (hardening sessions)

Merged 2026-10-15 from the former root file `PENDING_TASKS`. Tick here per session; the task rows are below.

**Legend:** ✅ = done, 🚧 = in progress, 💬 = waiting for owner, 🔲 = blocked, — = not started

## P0-Z. Consolidated open-work list (2026-10-16) — the only list to work from
This section is the one list of open work. Use it instead of the older sections below.
- Claude checked every status in code on 2026-10-16 (HEAD `1c1b6b6`). A status is not copied from an old file.
- The older sections below stay for history. They are superseded by this section.
- Counting rule: one row = one piece of work. A row that appears under other IDs lists every alias in its ID cell. The tag [A], [B] or [C] shows the source pass: A = review fix plan, B = design docs, logo, SEO and other plans, C = this file.
- A conflict stays visible as `CONFLICT: ...` in the status cell. Section Z.1 lists all conflicts. No conflict was resolved silently.
- Parked features (decided: park) sit in Z.4. Rows that only point to other rows (Suggested order) sit in Z.5 and are not counted.

## P0-A. University pilot — campus placement aptitude test (NEW)

> Superseded by § P0-Z (2026-10-16); rows kept for history.

Contact: Rajneesh, a professor at a university (college friend of the owner). He wants to trial AssessIQ for students' campus-placement aptitude tests. His email is in private memory, not here.

| # | Task | Why | Effort | Who |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| A1 | ✅ **DONE 2026-10-01** (`b18562f`, `ee9f8b7`; codex-reviewed, live). **Fix MCQ scoring (blocker).** Nothing creates MCQ grade rows. An MCQ-only attempt is never scored, never reaches `graded` and cannot be released. Mixed attempts silently drop MCQ points. Build deterministic MCQ scoring (selected option vs frozen `content.correct`, `grader='deterministic'`), run on the admin Grade/Finalize click, plus a finalize path for MCQ-only attempts. Correct the false "scored by module 09" comments. | Aptitude tests are almost all MCQ, so the pilot cannot work without this. Evidence: only writer is `07-ai-grading/src/repository.ts:204` (`insertGrading`); `admin-grade.ts:352-357` skips MCQ; `09-scoring/src/service.ts:35-130` only sums existing rows; release needs `graded` (`admin-claim-release.ts:362-382`). | M | Claude; load-bearing (06/07/09) so Opus review plus a codex gate |
| A2 | ✅ **DONE 2026-10-01** (`0c4f75c`, live). Questions were hand-authored and blind-checked, not AI-generated. **But see R1:** the answer keys are in public git history, so regenerate before a real drive. **Aptitude content.** Create platform domain "Aptitude" (Quantitative, Logical reasoning, Verbal). Generate an MCQ pack with AI generation, check the `generate-mcq` skill isn't security-flavoured, review, publish, and license it to the pilot tenant. | The app only has security domains (SOC, Phishing, Threat-intel). | S-M | Claude builds; owner reviews questions |
| A3 | **Create the pilot tenant** via super-admin "Create company" (needs fresh MFA) and invite Rajneesh as company admin. | Lets him log in and try it. Do it after A1 and A2 so he sees a working test. | S | Owner clicks (Claude can guide) |
| A4 | ✅ **DONE 2026-10-01** (`aff53c8`, live): Import from CSV with invite, capped at 200 per import. **Bulk student import.** Invites take existing `user_ids`, and CSV import is a 501 stub (`03-users/src/import.ts:11-15`, `apps/api/src/routes/admin-users.ts:151`). Build CSV (name, email) → create candidates → invite. | Adding a class of 100+ students one by one isn't workable. | M | Claude |
| A5 | 🟡 **Code side DONE 2026-10-01** (`def48bb`, `88b3b02`): bulk mail is retried for ~45 h instead of being dropped, sign-in codes are sent before bulk mail, SMTP fails fast (10 s/30 s timeouts). The plan upgrade is still the owner's call. **Email capacity.** Brevo free plan is 300/day, shared by 5 products. One class's invites plus results can use it all and block sign-in codes. ## P1-H. Public demo quiz — try without login (NEW 2026-10-01, for later)

> Superseded by § P0-Z (2026-10-16); rows kept for history.

| # | Task | Why | Effort | Who |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| H1 | ✅ **DONE 2026-10-02 (batch 4, live), `4e59b18` + `e426760`**: `/try` + `/try/certificate`, no network, OG image `/brand/social/try-og.png`; Caddy `@app` updated.** **Public "Try a sample test" demo** on assessiq.in, with no login and no account. About 6–8 questions covering several types (MCQ, a short subjective, a log/scenario item, a KQL-style item) plus a visible countdown timer, so it feels like the real exam: question navigator, autosave indicator, submit confirmation. At the end show a **final score** with a per-question breakdown and correct/dummy model answers, a link to a **sample certificate** (static demo credential on the real `/verify` page style), and **share options** (LinkedIn, X, WhatsApp, copy link, with an OG image). Scoring is deterministic and client- or server-side on fixed dummy answers: **no AI calls, no DB writes of candidate data, no email**. It must be rate-limit safe and must not create tenants or users. | Lets prospects feel the real exam before talking to sales. Competitors (HackerRank, TestGorilla, iMocha, Mercer Mettl) gate their demos behind signup. | M-L | Claude. Load `ui-design-workflow` plus the branding guideline first. Design bar: best-in-class, better than competitors'. Reuse the real candidate runner components (`11-candidate-ui`, `take/Attempt.tsx`) in a sandboxed demo mode, not a separate mock. |

## P0-S. Scoring and result-release plan (NEW 2026-10-01)

> Superseded by § P0-Z (2026-10-16); rows kept for history.

Plan: `docs/design/2026-10-01-scoring-and-result-release-plan.md`. Owner rules: students see **only a complete score** (never partial); if the result takes **more than about 1 minute**, tell the student it will be **emailed**; each tenant admin picks **Auto** or **Manual** release; grade with the **least AI** (answer keys → reuse/rules → AI → human). Replaces R3.

## P0-V. Fix plan from the 2026-10-02 full project review (NEW)

> Superseded by § P0-Z (2026-10-16); rows kept for history.

Source: a read-only review of all code, docs and features on 2026-10-02 (two passes, 9 review agents, direct checks, production row counts). Nothing is fixed yet. **Detail, evidence (file:line) and the fix for every item are in `docs/plans/REVIEW_FIX_PLAN_2026-10-02.md`** (local only; task IDs RV1-RV79, feature reviews PT1 and FR1-FR25, owner decisions RO1-RO7). Do the sessions in this order. Check each finding again before work: a second session changed code during the review.

**Two standing owner rules (2026-10-02). They apply to every task in this file, not only to this section:**

- **Rule A — keep dormant features.** Early-stage features were built for a reason. Do not delete a feature, screen or code path because it is stale. Mark it for review: first purpose, what operates today, is there a better option, can it be improved or joined with a newer feature. Result: revive, merge, improve or park. A delete is possible only when (a) detailed written research shows the feature is not needed, (b) it is absolutely useless or does not make sense on a professional product, and (c) the owner approves that item. Correcting untrue text that customers see is allowed.
- **Rule B — check old against new.** Before you implement any new task, look for a similar older task or feature (this file, `docs/plans`, `docs/design`, `docs/plans/archive/assessiq-skill-plan.md`, module `SKILL.md` files, help keys, the code). If one exists, review both and implement the better version: the old one, the new one, or a combination. Write in the task notes what you compared and why. Known pairs: plan file § 7.

**Owner decisions of 2026-10-02:** the product has **plan tiers** (not free; see D2, D3 and PT1); the **reviewer role is removed** to keep the product simple (RV60); **MFA for tenant admins stays optional now and is enabled later** (B4 stays open; all MFA code stays).

**Owner decisions raised by the review (2026-10-03).** Detail is in each review file, section "Owner decision needed".

- PT1-1 Tier contents: use the table in docs/plans/archive/RS6_FEATURE_REVIEW_A.md section PT1 (c), or fewer tiers? ✅ DECIDED 2026-10-03: Tiers: Starter (DB free), Growth (pro), Enterprise, plus internal, as in docs/plans/archive/RS6_FEATURE_REVIEW_A.md section PT1 (c).
- PT1-2 Credit meaning: keep one credit per graded attempt and add a second meter for AI evaluation? ✅ DECIDED 2026-10-03: One credit per graded attempt stays. A second meter counts AI evaluation.
- PT1-3 Monthly or lifetime credits (per-drive pack for campus drives)? ✅ DECIDED 2026-10-03: Monthly credits for Growth and Enterprise. A per-drive pack serves campus drives.
- PT1-4 INR prices (proposal only, not checked against the market). ✅ DECIDED 2026-10-03: INR ranges are a PROPOSAL ONLY and are NOT published. The pricing page keeps "contact sales" until the owner checks the market.
- PT1-5 Payment provider: Razorpay or Stripe (start with manual invoices and Razorpay payment links)? ✅ DECIDED 2026-10-03: Razorpay. Manual invoices and payment links first, subscriptions later.
- PT1-6 ✅ DONE 2026-10-03 (`e11cb5b`). Terms section 7: change "auto-renew" to "by order or contract" until a payment flow exists? ✅ DECIDED 2026-10-03: Terms section 7 now reads "renew only by a new order or as your contract states". DONE and LIVE 2026-10-03 (`e11cb5b`).
- PT1-7 Keep the 25-credit trial tier? ✅ DECIDED 2026-10-03: Keep the 25-credit trial.
- FR7 Use of the `ai_grading_enabled` pause switch in the platform queue (same family as PT1-2). ✅ DECIDED 2026-10-03: The `ai_grading_enabled` pause switch is part of the AI meter (with PT1-2).
- FR9 OD2: when to move to an API key; is BYOK a tier feature? ✅ DECIDED 2026-10-03: Park the API key. BYOK is an Enterprise feature.
- FR15 Is a tenant sender and white-label an Enterprise feature? When does the first request (I4) make it urgent? ✅ DECIDED 2026-10-03: Tenant sender and white-label are an Enterprise feature. Parked until the first request.
- FR19 Will anyone author questions in a spreadsheet? If no, CSV import stays parked. ✅ DECIDED 2026-10-03: No. CSV question authoring stays parked.
- FR1 Is the audit viewer and export a tier feature (suggestion: Enterprise)? Kit gap approval for the page. ✅ DECIDED 2026-10-03: The audit viewer and export are an Enterprise feature.
- FR2 First business events customers need (suggestion: `result.released`). Is webhooks a tier feature? Kit gap approval. ✅ DECIDED 2026-10-03: Webhooks are Growth and up. Events: `result.released`, `attempt.submitted`, `attempt.graded` (FU-B6 built 2026-10-03).
- FR3 Does any customer need server-to-server access (REST API tier)? If no, keep parked. ✅ DECIDED 2026-10-03: No customer now. Park the REST API.
- FR4 Is there a near customer for embed (for example the university portal)? Approval for any live Caddy header change. Tier placement. ✅ DECIDED 2026-10-03: No near customer. Do FU-B12 only. NO live Caddy header change. Tier: Enterprise.
- FR5 Confirm in-app notices first, email second. ✅ DECIDED 2026-10-03: In-app notices first, email second.
- FR6 Is a "we received your test" receipt email wanted (adds mail volume on big drives)? ✅ DECIDED 2026-10-03: No receipt email for now (mail volume).
- FR8 Show an expected time to tenant admins? Keep the menu name "Grading" or rename to "Evaluation status"? ✅ DECIDED 2026-10-03: Show an expected time (env text). The tenant menu name is "Evaluation status".
- FR10 Is a topic view useful for first customers? Keep leaderboard output admin-only? ✅ DECIDED 2026-10-03: Topic data merges into the cohort page. The leaderboard stays admin-only.
- FR11 Do tenant admins see the radar and archetype label? Wording: "recorded events", never "cheating". ✅ DECIDED 2026-10-03: Tenant admins see integrity counts only, worded "recorded events". No archetype label for tenants.
- FR12 Candidate accounts in every drive, or invitation links only? Decide together with R7. ✅ DECIDED 2026-10-03: Invitation links by default. Candidate accounts are decided together with R7.
- FR13 Confirm the three certificate tiers (completion, distinction, honors). ✅ DECIDED 2026-10-03: Three certificate tiers confirmed: completion, distinction, honors.
- FR20 Is the difficulty report platform-wide (super admin only)? What data volume starts Phase C? ✅ DECIDED 2026-10-03: Super admin only. Phase C starts at 30+ attempts per question.
- FR25 Reject or only warn on existing bad rubrics (after the row count is known)? ✅ DECIDED 2026-10-03: Warn on old rubrics, reject on new saves (built 2026-10-03).
- FR14 (OD-D1) Help editing: platform only, or tenants too? ✅ DECIDED 2026-10-03: Help editing: platform only.
- FR17 (OD-D2) Confirm tenant admins must not see queue data (recommended: super admin only). ✅ DECIDED 2026-10-03: Queue data: super admin only.
- FR18 (OD-D3) Keep Storybook as a dev tool and fund the upgrade after E13, or leave it parked? ✅ DECIDED 2026-10-03: Storybook stays parked until E13.
- FR21 (OD-D4) Is about 2 GB RAM for `assessiq-kusto` available on the shared VPS? ✅ DECIDED 2026-10-03: NO. The VPS has about 4 GB free of 7 GB and is shared (checked 2026-10-03).
- FR24 (OD-D5) Which tenants stay on `sharded`, and when is the Stage 3.1 flip allowed? ✅ DECIDED 2026-10-03: All tenants stay on `sharded`. The Stage 3.1 flip waits for E4 and a blessed eval.


**Owner decisions (detail in the plan file § 4):** RO1 ✅ plan tiers. RO2 ✅ replaced by Rule A (no removal; feature review). RO4 ✅ MFA optional now, enable later. RO5 and RO6 ✅ covered by Rule A (review as FR18 and FR6). RO7 ✅ remove the reviewer role. RO3 ✅ DECIDED 2026-10-03: keep "Organisation code" on the candidate login for now. **Still open:** public INR prices (a proposal only; the owner checks the market first, D2) and the payment flow build (D3: manual invoices and Razorpay payment links first).

SP10 says "not built" but release on the last accept is built. E10 says DONE but React 18, `ViewportLock` and "501" texts remain. B5 duplicates N5.

## P0-W. Hardening roadmap from the 2026-10-09 full review (NEW)

> Superseded by § P0-Z (2026-10-16); rows kept for history.

Source: read-only review 2026-10-09 (5 review agents: backend, AI pipeline, frontend, stale code, CI/infra; 4 page-by-page UI reviews; 2 independent verify passes; 1 cross-check against this file; 1 sweep of other docs for orphan tasks). Nothing is implemented yet. Hardening runs beside feature work; no feature freeze. Backup items excluded (weekly VPS backup exists). The AI runtime stays on the Claude Code CLI (no API).

**Findings (verified by read, not grep):**
- Size: ~127k lines app source (31k is the admin dashboard), 72k tests, 16k SQL, 89k markdown. The "300k" is mostly docs and tests.
- Strong: Fastify plugin per module, RLS with `SET LOCAL ROLE`, same-tx `auditInTx`, 13 custom lints, lean deps, test ratio 0.64.
- Weak backend: 9 modules without a repository; SQL inline in `apps/api/src/routes/admin-super/*`; 21 route files with no Zod; 33 `pool.connect()` sites outside `withTenant` and no `withSystemRole`; uuid regex x4, `parsePagination` x2, `escapeHtml` x2; 4 backend files over 1300 lines; no OpenAPI, no idempotency keys, no `/ready`.
- AI evaluation (not enterprise-grade yet): `AI_EVAL_GATE=warn`, no blessed baseline; single-flight is an in-process Map; grade-all runs in one HTTP request (CF 100 s timeout patched by `ai_proposals` + polling); tenant-pause re-check runs before the CLI call, not after; grades are auditable but not reproducible (no `cli_version`, no stage inputs); stub runtimes `anthropic-api` / `open-weights` and the omnibus/sharded toggle are dormant.
- Frontend: no i18n (the old i18n claim in `docs/01` was false); three API clients + 9 ad-hoc fetches; 13 TSX files over 1000 lines; 8 date-format approaches; 6 `window.confirm`, ~12 hand-rolled dialogs, 6 raw tables although the kit has `Modal`/`Table`; raw enums, UUID slices, SHAs and model names reach the UI; three nouns for one customer (company / organisation / tenant / workspace).
- Hygiene: 63 `worktree-agent-*` branches, 60 worktrees; `google-sso.ts.p1bak`; `BingTrackingCode.md`; 9 env toggles to confirm in `.env.example`; worker has no healthcheck; no container memory limits.
- Docs: 80 files / 42k lines; 32 plan files, 21 without closure; stale "reviewer" in 13 docs.
- Corrected during verify: audit CSV guard exists (RV77); ambient-AI lint runs in CI (`ci.yml:143`); timer has low-time states; Submit shows only on the last section; generation-attempts, stderr and platform-domain creation are already super-admin only; leaderboard ranks question sets, not candidates.

**Owner decisions that bind this section:** RO3 (keep "Organisation code"), FR14 (help editing platform-only), FU-C13 (Bloom/NICE chips stay), Rule A (park, never delete without review + approval), FR9 (API key parked), no backup work.

**Recommendations (order):** S1 to S10 in order. S3 (vocabulary) before any page work. God-file splits ride on whichever session touches the file. Routing: Haiku reads and verifies, Sonnet implements against the contract, Opus plans and critiques diffs, codex:rescue (or Sonnet takeover) gates `01`, `02`, `07`, `14`, `infra`.

Expected completion after S1 to S10: about 81% of this file (from 72% today). Remaining: owner-only rows and about 35 unscheduled older rows.

## P2-Logo. Org Logo Upload & Co-Branding (NEW 2026-10-09)

> Superseded by § P0-Z (2026-10-16); rows kept for history.

Owner decisions (locked 2026-10-09): Logo for all tenants (D2 ✅), next to company name + "via AssessIQ" (D1 ✅), current logo on certs (D4 ✅), no candidate login placement (D3 deferred). Design: `docs/design/2026-10-09-org-logo-feature.md`. Zero cost, 4–5 days, 25 files.

| # | Task | Why | Effort | Who |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Logo-L1 | ⏳ **Backend storage & API.** Create `tenant_logos` BYTEA table (RLS), validation (PNG/JPEG, 512 KB, magic bytes), functions (setTenantLogo, getTenantLogoBytes), endpoints (PUT/DELETE `/api/admin/tenant-settings/logo`, GET `/api/public/tenant-logo/<id>`), add `logo_url` to whoami, audit entries. Files: 7 (migrations, logo.ts, service.ts, routes). **Blocked by:** owner decisions ✅. **Gate:** codex:rescue (02-tenancy load-bearing). | Core storage for 8 surfaces (admin, candidate pages, emails, certs, verify, OG). Zero DB cost, existing backups cover it. | M | Claude; Sonnet + Opus review + codex gate |
| Logo-L2 | ⏳ **Admin panel.** File upload input, preview, remove button in tenant-settings; TenantLogo reusable component (28px, fallback to text); show in AdminShell header; help ID `admin.tenant_settings.logo`. Files: 6. **Blocked by:** Logo-L1 complete. | Org admins can upload their logo once, used everywhere. | S | Claude (Sonnet) |
| Logo-L3a | ⏳ **Candidate assessment pages.** Show logo on TokenLanding (100×40px, "via [Org]"), Attempt top bar (32×28px, co-brand), Submitted (60×40px, confirmation). Pass `company_logo_url` in token and attempt view. Files: 5. **Blocked by:** Logo-L1 complete. | Candidate sees org branding throughout test taking. | S | Claude (Sonnet) |
| Logo-L3b | ⏳ **Email branding.** Add logo header (120×48px) to all candidate emails: invitation, test started, result released, expiry warnings. Pass logoUrl (data URI) to email templates. Files: 5. **Blocked by:** Logo-L1 complete. | Org logo in every notification email. | S | Claude (Sonnet) |
| Logo-L3c | ⏳ **Verify page & OG social.** Show logo on `/verify/<id>` page (80×32px, "Issued by [Org]"), in OG social preview image for LinkedIn (240×96px, top-right), in certificate PDF (18mm top-left, data URI). Files: 2. **Blocked by:** Logo-L1 complete. | Public certificate and social share show org branding. | S | Claude (Sonnet) |
| Logo-L4 | ⏳ **Admin certificate PDF.** Embed logo in PDF template via data URI (18mm height, top-left), offline-safe. Files: 1 (already included in L3c). **Blocked by:** Logo-L1 complete. | Org logo on downloadable/printed certificates. | S | Claude (Sonnet, bundled with L3c) |

**MVP = L1 + L2 + L3a + L3b + L3c (4–5 days, 25 files).** L2–L3c can run in parallel after L1.

**Docs (all phases):** `docs/02-data-model.md` (table), `docs/03-api-contract.md` (endpoints), `docs/08-ui-system.md` (TenantLogo), `docs/10-branding-guideline.md` (amend section 2.11), `modules/18-certification/SKILL.md`.

**Displays (8 surfaces):** AdminShell (28px), admin settings preview (120px), TokenLanding (100×40px), Attempt (32×28px), Submitted (60×40px), emails (120×48px), verify page (80×32px), cert PDF (18mm), OG image (240×96px).

**Security:** Magic byte validation (no extension trust), RLS on table (tenant isolation), no stored URLs (computed from row), CSP sandbox on public GET, adminFreshMfa (15 min) on uploads.

**Timeline:** Session 1 = L1 (2 days, Sonnet impl + Opus review + codex gate). Session 2 = L2–L4 in 4 parallel agents (1 day each).
